# Records left out of the Research databases

Only events whose time is recorded exactly (to the minute, not reported or announced), with their date and place, are kept; a swearing-in also keeps a reported or approximate ceremony time. The rule is `dropped()` in server/mundane_data.py;
the source files are untouched, so a record comes back once its missing time is filled in.

| Set | Kept | Removed | Reasons |
|---|---:|---:|---|
| disasters | 4,173 | 6,032 | no time 3,569, time not exact 1,300, same as an earthquake 812, doubtful tsunami 224, before 1900 106, julian date 20, no place 1 |
| nations | 48 | 127 | no time 98, time not exact 27, julian date 2 |
| elections | 82 | 718 | no oath time 718 |
| sports | 1,193 | 423 | no time 418, time not exact 5 |
| events | 1,138 | 19 | julian date 19 |
